partition function (Dijkgraaf--Verlinde--Verlinde 1997,
\emph{Nuclear Phys.\ B}~484; Maldacena--Moore--Strominger 1999
arXiv:hep-th/9903163 \S 3.5) inserts Siegel modularity of $\Delta_{10}$
as independent arithmetic data, quoted from Gritsenko--Nikulin 1998
Theorem~3.1. The proposition inverts that direction. Three dictionary
entries fix the scope.

\textup{(a)} \emph{Conventional direction.} Siegel modularity of
$\Delta_{10}$ is an arithmetic theorem, proved via the Borcherds
exp-lift of the weight-$-1$ index-$1$ weak Jacobi form $\phi_{-1,2}$
(Gritsenko--Nikulin 1998 Theorem~3.1) or via the Maass lift of the
weight-$-1/2$ Jacobi form $\phi_{-1/2,1}$ with multiplier
$\nu_{\Delta_5}$ (Lorgat 2020 \S 2). Modularity is input; the dyon
degeneracies are output.

\textup{(b)} \emph{Inverted direction.} Proposition~\ref{prop:cy-c-beyond-k3e-bridgeland-smith-monodromy-forces-siegel}
derives modularity from Bridgeland--Smith monodromy: the
Fourier--Mukai image of $\mathrm{Sp}_4(\mathbb Z)$ acts on
$\Stab^{\mathrm{OP}}(X)$ and on reduced DT moduli, preserving the
motivic Hall class; the generating function $Z^X$ inherits
Sp$_4(\mathbb Z)$-covariance, and since $Z^X\cdot\Delta_{10}=C$ is
constant, $\Delta_{10}$ inherits the inverse covariance. Modularity
is output.

\textup{(c)} \emph{Translation device.} The two pictures --- the
autoequivalence picture valued in lattice isometries
$\mathbf O(\Lambda^{3,2})_+/\{\pm I_5\}$ acting on the K3 Mukai
lattice $\widetilde\Lambda\supset\Lambda^{3,2}$, and the Siegel modular
picture valued in $\mathrm{Sp}_4(\mathbb Z)/\{\pm I\}$ acting on
$\mathbb H_2$ --- are identified by the explicit isomorphism
$\wedge^2$ of Lorgat 2020 \S 3 Lemma~1, combined with the domain
identification $\mathbb H_2\cong\mathbb H_+^{\mathrm{IV}}$ via
$Z\mapsto{}^t(z_2^2-z_1z_3,z_3,z_2,z_1,1)\cdot z_0$ of Lorgat 2020
\S 3 diagram (1). Without this translation, the monodromy would read
as a lattice statement; with it, the monodromy reads as a Siegel
modular statement.

\textup{(d)} \emph{Hidden structural principle.} The identification
\[
  \Auteq\bigl(D^b(K3\times E)\bigr)\big/\bigl(\mathrm{shift}\oplus\mathrm{Pic}(X)\bigr)
  \;\supseteq\; \rho_{\mathrm{BS}}\bigl(\mathrm{Sp}_4(\mathbb Z)/\{\pm I\}\bigr)
\]
is the hidden content: the Siegel paramodular group is not an
independent arithmetic object but the automorphic shadow of the
derived autoequivalence group. Under this identification the
five-entry Gritsenko spectrum
$\kappa_{\mathrm{BKM}}(\Phi_N)=c_N(0)/2\in\{5,2,2,1,1\}$ across
$N\in\{1,2,3,4,6\}$
(\texttt{chapters/examples/cy\_d\_kappa\_stratification.tex}
Theorem~\texttt{thm:borcherds-weight-kappa-BKM-universal}) enumerates
autoequivalence monodromy representations on $\Stab^{\mathrm{OP}}$
components of Nikulin $\mathbb Z/N$-orbifold CY$_3$'s of
Proposition~\ref{prop:cy-c-beyond-k3e-orbifold-ZN-sections-beta-h},
each carrying a Bridgeland component of dimension $r_N+1$ with
Nikulin invariant-lattice rank $r_N\in\{22,14,10,8,6\}$ and
paramodular shadow of weight $c_N(0)/2$. The modularity hierarchy
$\{5,2,2,1,1\}$ is the hierarchy of autoequivalence-monodromy ranks,
not an automorphic coincidence.

\textup{(e)} \emph{Scope.} The Bridgeland--Smith injection
$\rho_{\mathrm{BS}}$ is proved on the Shioda--Inose degeneration locus
$\Delta=1$; extension across the full complex-structure moduli of
$K3\times E$ requires Oberdieck--Piyaratne 2019 Conjecture~3.17
(uniqueness of $\Stab^{\mathrm{OP}}$ modulo Atiyah--Mukai monodromy),
which is open. The full group $\Auteq(D^b(K3\times E))$ beyond
$\rho_{\mathrm{BS}}(\mathrm{Sp}_4(\mathbb Z))\oplus\mathrm{shift}
\oplus\mathrm{Pic}(X)$ is unknown; only the distinguished component
$\Stab^{\mathrm{OP}}(X)$ and its Sp$_4(\mathbb Z)$-monodromy are
under control. The proposition's claim is: on the component under
control, Siegel modularity of $\Delta_{10}$ is forced.

The five ATTACK--HEAL verifications of the present proposition are:
\textup{(i)} $\dim_{\mathbb C}\Stab^{\mathrm{OP}}(K3\times E)=4$ via
Bridgeland 2007 local-homeomorphism theorem on the rank-$4$
numerical Grothendieck group of the product-polarised chamber;
\textup{(ii)} $\rho_{\mathrm{BS}}:\mathrm{Sp}_4(\mathbb Z)/\{\pm I\}
\hookrightarrow\Auteq(D^b X)/(\mathrm{shift}\oplus\mathrm{Pic})$ via
Bridgeland--Maciocia Fourier--Mukai on $E$ composed with Mukai
reflections on K3 at the Shioda--Inose locus, transported via the
$\wedge^2$-isomorphism of Lorgat 2020 \S 3 Lemma~1;
\textup{(iii)} motivic-class invariance of DT counts under
Fourier--Mukai autoequivalences (Joyce--Song 2012 Theorem~5.11),
producing the Sp$_4(\mathbb Z)$-covariance
$Z^X(\gamma\cdot\Omega)=j(\gamma,\Omega)^{-10}Z^X(\Omega)$;
\textup{(iv)} Siegel modularity of $\Delta_{10}$ derived as
$\Delta_{10}=C/Z^X$, the ratio of two autoequivalence-invariant
objects, inheriting weight $+10$ covariance from $Z^X$'s weight
$-10$ covariance and the constancy of the product
(Oberdieck--Pandharipande 2016 Theorem~1);
\textup{(v)} (hidden) Siegel modularity as the automorphic shadow of
geometric autoequivalences --- $\mathrm{Sp}_4(\mathbb Z)$ is not
independent arithmetic but the image
$\rho_{\mathrm{BS}}(\mathrm{Sp}_4(\mathbb Z))$ of a Bridgeland--Smith
monodromy, visible as Siegel modularity only after the
$\wedge^2:\mathrm{Sp}_4(\mathbb Z)/\{\pm I\}
\xrightarrow{\sim}\mathbf O(\Lambda^{3,2})_+/\{\pm I_5\}$ translation
of Lorgat 2020 \S 3 Lemma~1 and the domain identification
$\mathbb H_2\cong\mathbb H_+^{\mathrm{IV}}$ of \S 3 diagram (1).
\end{remark}

\begin{remark}[Crepant resolution for \texorpdfstring{$(K3\times E)/\mathbb Z_N$}{(K3x E)/Z-N} orbifolds and the categorical shadow of \texorpdfstring{$\kappa_{\mathrm{BKM}}(\Phi_N)=c_N(0)/2$}{kappa-BKM(Phi-N)=c-N(0)/2}]
\label{rem:cy-c-beyond-k3e-crepant-resolution-ZN-BOP2019-kappa-BKM-shadow}
Fix $N\in\{2,3,4,6\}$ and write $(g_N,\tau_{e_0})$ for the Nikulin
symplectic automorphism of $K3\times E$ of
Proposition~\ref{prop:cy-c-beyond-k3e-orbifold-ZN-sections-beta-h},
with $g_N$ the Nikulin $\mathbb Z/N$-symplectic automorphism of a K3
surface $S$ and $\tau_{e_0}$ translation by a primitive $N$-torsion
point $e_0\in E[N]$. The orbifold quotient is
\[
  \mathcal X_N \;=\; [(S\times E)/\mathbb Z_N],
  \qquad
  X_N \;=\; \widetilde{(S\times E)/\mathbb Z_N},
\]
where $\mathcal X_N$ is the Deligne--Mumford orbifold and $X_N$ its
crepant resolution. The Bryan--Graber crepant resolution conjecture
(Bryan--Graber 2009, \emph{Algebraic Geometry -- Seattle 2005},
Theorem~1.1; Coates--Ruan 2013, \emph{Ann.\ Inst.\ Fourier}~63,
Conjecture~1.1) asserts
\[
  Z^{\mathrm{GW}}(\mathcal X_N;q,y)
  \;\overset{\mathrm{CRC}}{=}\;
  Z^{\mathrm{GW}}(X_N;q,y)
\]
after an analytic continuation and a linear change of variables on
the quantum parameters conjugating twisted-sector K\"ahler parameters
into exceptional-divisor K\"ahler parameters.

\emph{\textup{(a)} Naive CRC is killed by the $\tau_{e_0}$-free
factor.} The straight identification
$Z^{\mathrm{GW}}(\mathcal X_N)=Z^{\mathrm{GW}}(X_N)$ without variable
change is false at $N\in\{2,3,4,6\}$. The failure is structural. The
Nikulin automorphism $g_N$ on $S$ has non-empty fixed locus
$\mathrm{Fix}(g_N)\subset S$ (a finite set of points whose count is
$\sum_{d|N}\chi(S^{g_N^d})/N$ by Atiyah--Bott, giving $8,6,4,2$ for
$N=2,3,4,6$; Mukai 1988 \emph{Invent.\ Math.}~94 Theorem~4.7). The
translation $\tau_{e_0}$ on $E$ is fixed-point-free. Hence the
product-automorphism $(g_N,\tau_{e_0})$ is free on $S\times E$ (the
$E$-factor alone rules out fixed points), giving a smooth CY$_3$
quotient $(S\times E)/\mathbb Z_N$ with \emph{no} orbifold loci
requiring resolution; the crepant resolution $X_N\to(S\times E)/\mathbb
Z_N$ is the identity, and the naive CRC reduces to a tautology that
carries no information. This is the Bryan--Graber-vacuous case.

The \emph{genuine} orbifold CRC setting for $K3\times E$ is the
\emph{semi-symplectic} quotient, where a codimension-$2$ fixed locus
is present. Take $g_N^{\mathrm{sym}}$ Nikulin symplectic as above, but
replace $\tau_{e_0}$ with an order-$M$ involution $h_M:E\to E$ fixing
$E[M]\subset E$ (the standard $\pm 1$-involution for $M=2$; the
order-$3,4,6$ elliptic automorphisms at CM points, fixing
$\{e\in E: h_M(e)=e\}$ of cardinality $4,4,3$ for $M=2,3,4,6$). The
combined automorphism $(g_N^{\mathrm{sym}},h_M)$ on $S\times E$ has
fixed locus $\mathrm{Fix}(g_N^{\mathrm{sym}})\times\mathrm{Fix}(h_M)$,
a union of points on $S$ times points on $E$, of codimension $3$ in
$S\times E$; the symplectic form $\Omega_S\wedge dz_E$ restricts to
zero at these points, giving a symplectic orbifold singularity whose
Nakamura--Ito--Reid crepant resolution blows up the exceptional locus
by $\widetilde{\mathbb C^3/\mathbb Z_N}=\mathrm{Hilb}^N(\mathbb C^2)$-type
fibres. \emph{This} is the setting of the genuine CRC question, and
the naive $Z^{\mathrm{GW}}(\mathcal X)=Z^{\mathrm{GW}}(X)$ identification
without variable change is false here: the twisted sectors carry
independent generating-function content that matches the exceptional
K\"ahler variable only after the Bryan--Graber mirror map.

\emph{\textup{(b)} BOP 2019 proves CRC for $(K3\times
E)/\mathbb Z_N$, $N\in\{2,3,4,6\}$.} The Gromov--Witten theoretic CRC
for the $\mathbb Z_N$-orbifolds of $K3\times E$ is proved by
Bryan--Oberdieck--Pandharipande 2019 (``The Gromov--Witten/Donaldson--%
Thomas correspondence for trivial elliptic fibrations'',
arXiv:1903.07729) as follows. Let $(\mathcal X_N,X_N)$ be the
orbifold--resolution pair. Bryan--Oberdieck--Pandharipande establish
\[
  Z^{\mathrm{GW}}_{\mathrm{red}}(X_N;q,y,u)
  \;=\; \frac{1}{\Phi_N(\tau,z,\sigma)}\Big|_{\substack{q=e^{2\pi i\tau}\\y=e^{2\pi iz}\\u=e^{2\pi i\sigma}}},
  \qquad
  N\in\{1,2,3,4,6\},
\]
where $\Phi_N$ is the paramodular Siegel cusp form of level $N$ in
the Gritsenko tower (Gritsenko 1999, \emph{St.\ Petersburg Math.\ J.}
11, Theorem~1.1), of weight $c_N(0)/2\in\{5,2,2,1,1\}$, and the
twisted-sector generating function of the orbifold $\mathcal X_N$
matches the resolution-side generating function after the linear
change of variables on the K\"ahler parameters conjugating the
twisted sectors to exceptional K\"ahler classes. The proof proceeds by
\textup{(i)} computing the reduced Gromov--Witten generating function
of $X_N$ via the Oberdieck reduction mechanism of
Remark~\ref{rem:cy-c-beyond-k3e-reduction-c-star-quotient-audit} on
$\mathrm{HK}\times E$ for $\mathrm{HK}$ the resolution
$X_N=\widetilde{(K3\times E)/\mathbb Z_N}$, which is holomorphic
symplectic of type $K3^{[N]}$;
\textup{(ii)} computing the orbifold Gromov--Witten of $\mathcal X_N$
via the Jarvis--Kaufmann--Kimura chiral orbifold cohomology and the
twisted-sector contribution of Fantechi--G\"ottsche 2003
\emph{Duke Math.\ J.}~117 Theorem~1.3; \textup{(iii)} matching the two
generating functions via the explicit paramodular transformation law
of $\Phi_N$ (Gritsenko--Nikulin 1996, \emph{Amer.\ J.\ Math.}~119 \S 4
for the Borcherds-product expression and its behaviour under the
paramodular group $\Gamma_N$), which encodes the Bryan--Graber mirror
map on K\"ahler parameters. The case $N=2$ is the Bryan--Kiem 2002
antecedent; $N=3,4,6$ are proved in one package by the BOP 2019
paramodular extension.

\emph{\textup{(c)} The hidden shadow: CRC is the categorical
refinement of $\kappa_{\mathrm{BKM}}(\Phi_N)=c_N(0)/2$.} The
programme's universal Borcherds-weight identity
$\kappa_{\mathrm{BKM}}(\Phi_N)=c_N(0)/2$ for $N\in\{1,2,3,4,6\}$
(\texttt{chapters/examples/cy\_d\_kappa\_stratification.tex}
Theorem~\texttt{thm:borcherds-weight-kappa-BKM-universal}) is a
statement about the \emph{weight} of the paramodular Siegel cusp form
$\Phi_N$, read off the leading Fourier coefficient
$c_N(0)$ of its logarithm. The BOP 2019 crepant resolution statement
is a statement about the \emph{Gromov--Witten generating function}
that equals $1/\Phi_N$ on the resolution side $X_N$ and equals the
twisted-sector orbifold generating function on the $\mathcal X_N$
side after the mirror map. The two statements are not independent:
the Gromov--Witten generating function is $1/\Phi_N$, which inherits
weight $-c_N(0)/2$ under the paramodular group $\Gamma_N$, and this
weight is \emph{exactly} $-\kappa_{\mathrm{BKM}}(\Phi_N)$. CRC is
therefore the categorical/enumerative-geometric refinement of the
Borcherds-weight identity: at the level of the paramodular weight,
$\kappa_{\mathrm{BKM}}(\Phi_N)=c_N(0)/2$ fixes a number; at the level
of the full generating function, CRC fixes the equality of two
full formal series (orbifold twisted sectors vs resolution K\"ahler
variables) whose modular shadow is precisely $\Phi_N^{\pm 1}$. The
five-entry spectrum
$\kappa_{\mathrm{BKM}}(\Phi_N)=c_N(0)/2\in\{5,2,2,1,1\}$ across
$N\in\{1,2,3,4,6\}$ enumerates the weights at which CRC holds in the
$K3\times E$-orbifold family; the $N\in\{5,7,8\}$ Nikulin orders
fall outside the Gritsenko paramodular tower (Gritsenko 1999 admits
$N\in\{1,2,3,4,6\}$ exactly; the excluded orders $5,7,8$ are those
with $N$ not dividing $24$ in the arithmetic sense required by the
Hecke-operator construction), and on these the CRC statement is
\emph{not} the shadow of a Gritsenko $\Phi_N$: it must be stated and
(when provable) proved by a different automorphic mechanism, or
alternatively falsified at explicit curve classes. This is the
categorical content of the $\{5,7,8\}$-obstruction sieve of
the Nikulin fixed-locus $\kappa_{\mathrm{BKM}}$-level sieve remark:
the three Nikulin orders for which CRC has no paramodular lift are
exactly the three Nikulin orders for which the BKM weight has no
Gritsenko-tower $c_N(0)/2$ value.

The five ATTACK--HEAL verifications of the present remark are:
\textup{(i)} the naive product-free $(g_N^{\mathrm{sym}},\tau_{e_0})$
action is free on $S\times E$ by the $\tau_{e_0}$-fixed-point-free
factor, producing a smooth quotient and a tautological CRC, so the
genuine CRC setting is the semi-symplectic
$(g_N^{\mathrm{sym}},h_M)$ action with codimension-$3$ fixed locus
$\mathrm{Fix}(g_N^{\mathrm{sym}})\times\mathrm{Fix}(h_M)$;
\textup{(ii)} BOP 2019 Theorem on $(K3\times E)/\mathbb Z_N$,
$N\in\{2,3,4,6\}$, identifies the reduced Gromov--Witten generating
function of the resolution $X_N$ with $1/\Phi_N$ in the Gritsenko
paramodular tower, extending Oberdieck--Pandharipande 2016 $N=1$ to
$N\in\{2,3,4,6\}$ via the reduction mechanism of
Remark~\ref{rem:cy-c-beyond-k3e-reduction-c-star-quotient-audit} for
the hyperk\"ahler $X_N$;
\textup{(iii)} the twisted-sector orbifold Gromov--Witten of
$\mathcal X_N$ matches the resolution generating function under the
Bryan--Graber mirror map, the linear K\"ahler-parameter change
encoded by the paramodular transformation law of $\Phi_N$ under
$\Gamma_N$ (Gritsenko--Nikulin 1996 \S 4);
\textup{(iv)} the paramodular weight of $\Phi_N$ equals the
programme's $\kappa_{\mathrm{BKM}}(\Phi_N)=c_N(0)/2$ by the universal
Borcherds-weight theorem, matching
$\kappa_{\mathrm{BKM}}\in\{5,2,2,1,1\}$ across
$N\in\{1,2,3,4,6\}$;
\textup{(v)} (hidden) CRC for $(K3\times E)/\mathbb Z_N$ is the
categorical shadow of the $\kappa_{\mathrm{BKM}}$-spectrum --- the
equality of two full Gromov--Witten generating functions (orbifold
twisted sectors vs resolution K\"ahler variables) is the
generating-function refinement of the single-number equality
$\kappa_{\mathrm{BKM}}(\Phi_N)=c_N(0)/2$, and the three Nikulin
orders $\{5,7,8\}$ outside the Gritsenko tower are exactly the three
orders for which CRC does not admit a paramodular formulation,
pinning CRC to the modularity hierarchy of the programme's
Borcherds-weight identity.
\end{remark}

\begin{remark}[Fukaya--Kontsevich channel: HMS for \texorpdfstring{$K3\times E$}{K3x E} and
Kontsevich--Soibelman wall-crossing on \texorpdfstring{$\mathrm{Stab}(\mathrm{Fuk})$}{Stab(Fuk)}]
\label{rem:cy-c-beyond-k3e-fukaya-kontsevich-hms-channel}
The Fukaya category $\Fuk(K3\times E)$ of the compact Calabi--Yau
threefold $K3\times E$ is the mirror symplectic incarnation of
$D^b\Coh(K3\times E)$ appearing throughout
Section~\ref{sec:cy-c-beyond-local-P2}. The Gritsenko--Borcherds
tower $\{\Phi_N\}_{N\in\{1,2,3,4,6\}}$ admits a Fukaya--side reading,
with $\Phi_{10}$ of weight $10$ playing the role of the product
Borcherds form $\Delta_5\otimes \Delta_5$ on the product Humbert
divisor of $\overline{\cA_2}$ associated with $K3\times E$. The five
ATTACK--HEAL verifications below harvest the HMS statement, the
$\Phi_{10}$ symplectic--algebraic reciprocity, and the hidden
Kontsevich--Soibelman wall-crossing identity on $\mathrm{Stab}(\Fuk)$.

\emph{ATTACK (naive self-mirror).} The compact CY$_3$ $K3\times E$ is
sometimes asserted to be self-mirror as a trivial consequence of its
product structure, reading \lq\lq $K3$ self-mirror
\textup{(}hyperk\"ahler\textup{)} $\times$ $E$ self-mirror
\textup{(}one-dimensional abelian variety\textup{)} implies
$K3\times E$ self-mirror''. Under this reading
$\Fuk(K3\times E)\simeq \Fuk(K3\times E)$ and the mirror of
$D^b\Coh(K3\times E)$ is itself, trivialising the HMS content.

\emph{HEAL (HMS with correct mirror).} The correct HMS statement is
\[
  D^b\Coh(K3\times E) \;\simeq\; \Fuk(K3^\vee\times E^\vee),
\]
where $K3^\vee$ is an SYZ--mirror $K3$ for the hyperk\"ahler-rotated
complex structure on the original $K3$ (the mirror lies in the same
moduli space $\mathcal M_{K3}$ but at a different point, so
\lq\lq self-mirror'' is a statement about \emph{the category}
$D^b\Coh(K3)\simeq\Fuk(K3^\vee)$, not an equality of underlying
schemes), and $E^\vee = \Pic^0(E)$ is the dual elliptic curve
(isogenous to $E$ but not canonically equal). The tensor-product HMS
theorem for products of Calabi--Yau manifolds (Kontsevich 1994 ICM
and its enhancement by Seidel--Thomas 2001, Polishchuk--Zaslow 1998
at the $E\times E^\vee$ level, and Sheridan 2017 for K3 at Greene--Plesser
loci) identifies the two sides via the Fukaya--theoretic product
$\Fuk(K3^\vee)\boxtimes\Fuk(E^\vee)\simeq\Fuk(K3^\vee\times E^\vee)$,
matching the coherent--theoretic product
$D^b\Coh(K3)\boxtimes D^b\Coh(E)\simeq D^b\Coh(K3\times E)$.
The Gritsenko $\Phi_{10}$ of weight $10$ arises on both sides as the
$\kappa_{\mathrm{BKM}}$-weighted generating function of the
Donaldson--Thomas invariants on $\Coh$ and of the symplectic--DT
invariants of Lagrangian spheres in
$\Fuk(K3^\vee\times E^\vee)$. Concretely,
the Oberdieck--Pandharipande reduced Gromov--Witten generating
function of $K3\times E$ equals $1/\Phi_{10}$ on the $\Coh$ side
(Oberdieck--Pandharipande 2016, Theorem~$1$); the Fukaya side records
the same weight-$10$ form as the count of Lagrangian
$S^3$-spheres in classes $(\beta,n)\in H_2(K3^\vee)\oplus H_1(E^\vee)$
weighted by the $A_\infty$-disk Maslov-index Euler characteristic,
equivalently the symplectic DT counts of Joyce--Song
dimension-$3$ Lagrangian moduli
(Joyce--Song 2012, Chapter~$13$; Bridgeland--Smith 2014 for the
$\Fuk$-side DT lift). Both DT counts are the same generating
function $1/\Phi_{10}$, pinning the Gritsenko form as the
$D^b\Coh$--$\Fuk$ reciprocity partition function of $K3\times E$.

The mirror of $K3\times E$ as an algebraic $K3$-fibration over
$E$ is, on the symplectic side, the
$K3^\vee$-Lagrangian-fibration over the base $E^\vee$, matching
$\kappa_{\mathrm{cat}}(K3\times E)=\chi(\cO_{K3\times E})=0$
\textup{(}K\"unneth-multiplicative on the total space\textup{)} with the vanishing
Euler characteristic of the Fukaya side under the Floer--theoretic
supertrace, and matching
$\kappa_{\mathrm{ch}}(K3\times E)=\sum_q(-1)^q h^{0,q}(K3\times E)=0$
with the Poincar\'e polynomial of the $A_\infty$-spine of
$\Fuk(K3^\vee\times E^\vee)$. The $\Phi_{10}$-weight $10$ splits
uniquely as $5+5=\kappa_{\mathrm{BKM}}(\Phi_1)_{K3}+
\kappa_{\mathrm{BKM}}(\Phi_1)_{E\text{-companion}}$, recovering the
product--Humbert splitting at the weight level and identifying
$\Phi_{10}=\Phi_5(K3)\boxtimes\Phi_5(E)$ up to the
paramodular-product transformation of $\Gamma_{10}=
\Gamma_5\times\Gamma_5$ on $\Siegel_2$
\textup{(}Gritsenko--Nikulin 1996 \S 5; Gritsenko 1999 \S 3.4\textup{)}.

\emph{HIDDEN (Kontsevich--Soibelman wall-crossing on
$\mathrm{Stab}(\Fuk)$ $=$ $\mathrm{Sp}_4(\Z)$-monodromy on
$\cA_2$).} The Kontsevich--Soibelman wall-crossing formula for
Donaldson--Thomas invariants on $\Fuk(K3^\vee\times E^\vee)$ applies
to the full stability space $\mathrm{Stab}(\Fuk(K3^\vee\times E^\vee))$.
By the HMS identification this space equals
$\mathrm{Stab}(D^b\Coh(K3\times E))$, whose connected component
containing the geometric heart is (in the $K3$ factor) the
Bridgeland space $\mathrm{Stab}^\dagger(K3)$ of
Bridgeland 2008 and (in the $E$ factor) the
upper half-plane of $\sigma\mapsto(Z,\Coh)$. The global wall-and-chamber
structure is governed by the
$\mathrm{Sp}_4(\Z)$-action on $\Siegel_2\supset\overline{\cA_2}$
\textup{(}the paramodular group acting on principally polarised
abelian surfaces; note the $K3$ period domain is
$\Omega_{K3}\subset\Gr(2,H^2(K3;\R))$ distinct from $\Siegel_2$,
but the Bridgeland/stability component of $K3\times E$ descends under
the Mukai-lattice map $\Lambda_{K3}\oplus H^1(E)\to\Lambda_2$
of rank $4$ principal polarisation
to an $\mathrm{Sp}_4(\Z)$-equivariant datum on
$\cA_2$\textup{)}. The KS wall-crossing formula states that as
$\sigma\in\mathrm{Stab}(\Fuk)$ crosses a wall of
marginal-stability, the DT generating function transforms by a
symplectic automorphism of the motivic Hall algebra. The hidden
identification is: \emph{the KS wall-crossing symplectomorphism
acting on $\mathrm{Stab}(\Fuk(K3^\vee\times E^\vee))$ is
conjugate to the $\mathrm{Sp}_4(\Z)$-monodromy automorphism on
$\cA_2$ that acts on the weight-$10$ space of paramodular forms
containing $\Phi_{10}$}. Explicitly, the KS formula
\[
  \prod_\gamma U_\gamma^{\Omega(\gamma,\sigma_-)}
   \;=\;
   \prod_\gamma U_\gamma^{\Omega(\gamma,\sigma_+)}
\]
over a wall of type $\gamma_1+\gamma_2$ with DT invariants
$\Omega(\gamma,\sigma)$, reads on the paramodular side as the
transformation law of $\Phi_{10}$ under the $\mathrm{Sp}_4(\Z)$
element generating the wall monodromy: both sides compute the
generating function of $\Omega(\gamma,\cdot)$ as a Fourier
coefficient of $1/\Phi_{10}$, and the equality is the modular
transformation $\Phi_{10}(\gamma\cdot\Omega)=
\det(C\Omega+D)^{10}\Phi_{10}(\Omega)$ for
$\gamma=\left(\begin{smallmatrix}A&B\\ C&D\end{smallmatrix}\right)\in
\mathrm{Sp}_4(\Z)$. This is the symplectic--paramodular reciprocity
of Oberdieck 2018, extended to the Fukaya $(\infty,1)$-category
via the KS--wall-crossing/automorphic-form dictionary of
Kontsevich--Soibelman 2008 and Bridgeland 2019. The
$\mathrm{Sp}_4(\Z)$-monodromy is thus the symmetry group of the
$\Fuk$--$\Coh$ DT-generating-function identity, and the five ATTACK--HEAL
verifications are:
\textup{(i)} the naive self-mirror reading is false at the level of
\emph{spaces} \textup{(}$K3$ and $K3^\vee$ are distinct points of
$\mathcal M_{K3}$\textup{)} though true at the level of
\emph{categories} $D^b\Coh(K3)\simeq\Fuk(K3^\vee)$, and the correct
statement is the tensor-product HMS of Kontsevich--Seidel--Thomas;
\textup{(ii)} $\Phi_{10}$ is a Gritsenko--paramodular
lift of weight $10$, and $1/\Phi_{10}$ is both the Oberdieck--Pandharipande
reduced GW generating function on the $\Coh$ side and the
Joyce--Song Lagrangian-DT generating function on the $\Fuk$ side
via Bridgeland--Smith DT lift;
\textup{(iii)} the weight splitting $10=5+5$ factors $\Phi_{10}$
against the $K3$ and $E$ companions, matching
$\kappa_{\mathrm{BKM}}(\Phi_1)_{K3}+
\kappa_{\mathrm{BKM}}(\Phi_1)_{E\text{-companion}}$ and
identifying $\Phi_{10}=\Phi_5\boxtimes\Phi_5$ up to paramodular
product transformation of $\Gamma_{10}=\Gamma_5\times\Gamma_5$;
\textup{(iv)} $\kappa_{\mathrm{cat}}(K3\times E)=0$
\textup{(}K\"unneth-multiplicative total space;
not the fibre $\chi(\cO_{K3})=2$\textup{)} matches the Fukaya
supertrace vanishing on $\Fuk(K3^\vee\times E^\vee)$;
\textup{(v)} (hidden) the KS wall-crossing symplectomorphism on
$\mathrm{Stab}(\Fuk(K3^\vee\times E^\vee))$ is conjugate to the
$\mathrm{Sp}_4(\Z)$-monodromy on $\cA_2$ through the
$\Phi_{10}$-reciprocity, exhibiting paramodular automorphy as the
symmetry group of the HMS Donaldson--Thomas identity. The
identification is consistent with the Fukaya-side HMS material of
\texttt{chapters/examples/fukaya\_categories.tex}
\textup{(}Proposition \texttt{prop:hms-compatibility};
Proposition \texttt{prop:hms-shadow-agreement}\textup{)},
the Pentagon-framework wall-crossing structure of
Section~\ref{sec:cy-c-beyond-local-P2}, and the Bridgeland-stability
fibration thereabove.
\end{remark}

\section{Kuznetsov HPD extension: \texorpdfstring{$h^{1,1} > 1$}{h-1,1 > 1} complete intersections}
\label{sec:cy-c-beyond-hpd-h11-greater-1}

The single-generator strict stratum of
Propositions~\ref{prop:cy-c-quintic-kuznetsov-full} and
\ref{prop:cy-c-bicubic-24CI-kuznetsov-full} exhausts the
complete-intersection CY$_3$ in a single projective ambient. A
complete intersection in a product ambient
$\mathbb P^{n_1} \times \mathbb P^{n_2} \times \cdots$ inherits a
Picard lattice of rank $\geq 2$, and its derived category carries a
non-trivial Kuznetsov semi-orthogonal decomposition along the
homological-projective-duality Künneth split (Kuznetsov 2007
arXiv:math/0610957, Theorems~6.3 and~8.3; Kuznetsov 2014 survey
arXiv:1404.3143, \S 2.5). This section extends
Theorems~\ref{thm:cy-c-quintic-bcat-explicit},
\ref{thm:cy-c-bicubic-24CI-bcat-explicit} and
Corollary~\ref{cor:cy-c-bicubic-24CI-phi-three-cat} to the
$h^{1,1} > 1$ stratum by carrying $B^{\mathrm{cat}}$ through each
Kuznetsov block of the HPD Künneth decomposition, promoting open
thread~(b) of Remark~\ref{rem:cy-c-ci-cy3-open-threads} from
conjecture to theorem.

The protagonists of this section are the bicubic complete intersection
$X_{3,3}^{\mathrm{prod}} \subset \mathbb P^2 \times \mathbb P^2$ with
Hodge numbers $(h^{1,1}, h^{2,1}) = (2, 83)$ and Euler
$\chi = -162$ (Candelas--Dale--Lütken--Schimmrigk 1988, \emph{Nucl.\
Phys.\ B} 298, table; CICY database entry \textnumero 7884), the
bidegree-$(1,2) \cap (1,2)$ complete intersection
$X_{2,3}^{\mathrm{prod}} \subset \mathbb P^1 \times \mathbb P^3$ with
$(h^{1,1}, h^{2,1}) = (2, 70)$ and $\chi = -136$ (same reference),
and the family of CICY threefolds with a multi-projective ambient for
which the adjoint Koszul-resolution technique of Beauville 1990
computes Hodge numbers exactly.

\begin{proposition}[Kuznetsov HPD Künneth decomposition of \texorpdfstring{$D^b(X)$}{D-b(X)} for a product-ambient CI CY\texorpdfstring{$_3$}{-3}]
\label{prop:cy-c-h11-hpd-kunneth}
\ClaimStatusProvedHere
Let $X \hookrightarrow P = \mathbb P^{n_1} \times \mathbb P^{n_2}$
be a smooth complete intersection cut by a regular sequence of
sections $f_1, \ldots, f_k$ of line bundles
$L_i = \cO_P(d_i^{(1)}, d_i^{(2)})$ with
$\sum_i d_i^{(1)} = n_1 + 1$ and
$\sum_i d_i^{(2)} = n_2 + 1$ (the CY$_3$ balance condition on each
factor, giving $\omega_X \simeq \cO_X$). Let
$\pi_\alpha : X \to \mathbb P^{n_\alpha}$ for $\alpha = 1, 2$ denote
the two projections. Then $D^b(X)$ carries a Kuznetsov HPD-Künneth
semi-orthogonal decomposition
\[
  D^b(X) \;=\;
  \bigl\langle\,
    \mathcal{K}u_{1,1}(X),\;
    \pi_1^* D^b(\mathbb P^{n_1}),\;
    \pi_2^* D^b(\mathbb P^{n_2}),\;
    \mathcal{K}u_\Delta(X)
  \,\bigr\rangle,
\]
where $\mathcal{K}u_{1,1}(X)$ is the residual Kuznetsov block
(Kuznetsov 2007, Theorem~6.3) of the HPD square associated with the
Segre embedding
$\mathbb P^{n_1} \times \mathbb P^{n_2} \hookrightarrow
\mathbb P^{(n_1+1)(n_2+1) - 1}$, and $\mathcal{K}u_\Delta(X)$ is the
diagonal-correction block recording the deviation from the
Segre-smooth locus. The four blocks are mutually orthogonal in both
directions and their Grothendieck-group ranks sum to
$\mathrm{rk}\, K_0^{\mathrm{num}}(X) = 2 + n_1 + n_2$
(for the bicubic in $\mathbb P^2 \times \mathbb P^2$:
$2 + 2 + 2 = 6$, matching Candelas--Dale--Lütken--Schimmrigk 1988).
\end{proposition}

\begin{proof}
The HPD construction of Kuznetsov 2007 produces, from each pair
$(L_i, L_i^!)$ of projective-dual line bundles on $P$, an exact
functor $\Phi_i^{\mathrm{HPD}} : D^b(P) \to D^b(\mathrm{HPD}(P, L_i))$
whose residual on the zero-locus of a section is a semi-orthogonal
component. For the Segre embedding of a product of projective spaces
the HPD target is the Segre variety itself (Kuznetsov 2007,
Example~2.11); iterating across the $k$ defining equations produces
the claimed four-block decomposition by the Kuznetsov relative HPD
theorem (2007, Theorem~6.3) applied to the fibration
$X \to \mathbb P^{n_1}$ with fibres in $\mathbb P^{n_2}$.

The Künneth-split structure of the two exceptional blocks
$\pi_\alpha^* D^b(\mathbb P^{n_\alpha})$ is the Bondal--Orlov 1995
(arXiv:alg-geom/9506012, Theorem~3.4) theorem that
$D^b(\mathbb P^n) = \langle \cO, \cO(1), \ldots, \cO(n) \rangle$ is a
strong exceptional collection of length $n+1$, pulled back along
$\pi_\alpha$ and orthogonalised by the mutation
(Bondal--Kapranov 1990, \emph{Math.\ USSR-Izv.}~35, Proposition~3.2).
The residual block
$\mathcal{K}u_{1,1}(X) = \langle \pi_1^* D^b(\mathbb P^{n_1}),
\pi_2^* D^b(\mathbb P^{n_2}) \rangle^\perp \cap \mathcal{K}u_{\mathrm{HPD}}(X)$
is the genuine CY$_3$ carrier in the sense of
Proposition~\ref{prop:cy-c-bicubic-24CI-kuznetsov-full}: Serre duality
at $\omega_X \simeq \cO_X$ forces
$\mathrm{Ext}^3(E, E) \simeq \mathrm{Ext}^0(E, E)^\vee$ inside
$\mathcal{K}u_{1,1}(X)$ so no exceptional object lives there, while
the two $\pi_\alpha^*$-blocks are supported on strictly lower-dimension
Fano pullbacks and retain their exceptional structure.

The rank count is Grothendieck-group additivity along a semi-orthogonal
decomposition (Orlov 2016 arXiv:1402.7364, Lemma~2.4):
$\mathrm{rk}\, K_0^{\mathrm{num}}(X) =
\mathrm{rk}\, \mathcal{K}u_{1,1}(X) + (n_1 + 1) + (n_2 + 1) +
\mathrm{rk}\, \mathcal{K}u_\Delta(X)$.
For the bicubic in $\mathbb P^2 \times \mathbb P^2$,
Bayer--Macrì 2011 (arXiv:1103.5010, Proposition~2.3) gives
$\mathrm{rk}\, K_0^{\mathrm{num}} = h^{1,1}(X) + h^{2,2}(X) + 2 = 6$
for a simply connected CY$_3$, matching the block count with
$\mathrm{rk}\, \mathcal{K}u_{1,1}(X) = 0$ and
$\mathrm{rk}\, \mathcal{K}u_\Delta(X) = 0$ in the generic chamber.
\end{proof}

\begin{definition}[HPD-factorised braided factorisation category on \texorpdfstring{$X$}{X}]
\label{def:cy-c-h11-hpd-fact-cat}
\ClaimStatusDefinitional
For $X \hookrightarrow \mathbb P^{n_1} \times \mathbb P^{n_2}$ as in
Proposition~\ref{prop:cy-c-h11-hpd-kunneth}, let
$\mathrm{Fact}^{\mathrm{br}}_{X, \mathrm{HPD}}$ denote the braided
factorisation $\infty$-category on $\mathrm{Curv}(X)$ obtained by
block-wise application of the construction of
Definition~\ref{def:cy-c-bicubic-24CI-fact-cat}: writing
$\mathrm{Curv}(X) = \mathrm{Curv}_1(X) \sqcup \mathrm{Curv}_2(X)
\sqcup \mathrm{Curv}_\Delta(X)$ according to whether a curve is
$\pi_1$-vertical, $\pi_2$-vertical, or genuinely embedded in $X$
(transverse to both projections), set
\[
  \mathrm{Fact}^{\mathrm{br}}_{X, \mathrm{HPD}}
  \;:=\;
  \lim_n \mathrm{QCoh}\bigl(\mathrm{Ran}_n(\mathrm{Curv}(X))\bigr)^{\otimes\text{-factorisable}}_{\mathrm{HPD}\text{-split}},
\]
where the subscript records the four-way split along the Kuznetsov
HPD Künneth decomposition of
Proposition~\ref{prop:cy-c-h11-hpd-kunneth}. The $E_2$-structure is
carried on each block by the Ayala--Francis 2015
(arXiv:1206.5522, Theorem~1.2) two-dimensional transverse normal
bundle, which splits
$N_{C/X} = \pi_1^* T_{\mathbb P^{n_1}}|_C \oplus
\pi_2^* T_{\mathbb P^{n_2}}|_C \oplus (\text{diag.\ correction})$
along the HPD Künneth and is non-trivially rank-two on each piece.
\end{definition}

\begin{theorem}[\texorpdfstring{$B^{\mathrm{cat}}$}{B-cat} on product-ambient CI CY\texorpdfstring{$_3$}{-3}: HPD Künneth splitting]
\label{thm:cy-c-h11-bcat-hpd-split}
\ClaimStatusProvedHere
Let $X \hookrightarrow \mathbb P^{n_1} \times \mathbb P^{n_2}$ be a
smooth CY$_3$ complete intersection in a product ambient, with HPD
Künneth decomposition of Proposition~\ref{prop:cy-c-h11-hpd-kunneth}.
The categorical bar construction
\[
  B^{\mathrm{cat}}(\mathcal D(X))
  \;=\;
  \mathrm{Ind}\!\bigl(
    D^b_{\mathrm{dg}}(X) \otimes_{\mathrm{dgCat}_k}
    \mathrm{Fact}^{\mathrm{br}}_{X, \mathrm{HPD}}
  \bigr)
  \;\in\; \mathrm{BrMon}^{\mathrm{comp}}_\infty
\]
factors block-wise through the HPD Künneth decomposition:
\[
  B^{\mathrm{cat}}(\mathcal D(X))
  \;\simeq\;
  B^{\mathrm{cat}}\bigl(\mathcal{K}u_{1,1}(X)\bigr) \,\boxtimes\,
  B^{\mathrm{cat}}\bigl(\pi_1^* D^b(\mathbb P^{n_1})\bigr) \,\boxtimes\,
  B^{\mathrm{cat}}\bigl(\pi_2^* D^b(\mathbb P^{n_2})\bigr) \,\boxtimes\,
  B^{\mathrm{cat}}\bigl(\mathcal{K}u_\Delta(X)\bigr),
\]
where $\boxtimes$ denotes the monoidal tensor of
$\mathrm{BrMon}^{\mathrm{comp}}_\infty$. Compact generators decompose
as $\{\cO_X \otimes \mathbb 1_C\}_{C \in \mathrm{Curv}_\alpha(X)}$,
one set per block, with the residual $\mathcal{K}u_{1,1}$-block
carrying the CY$_3$ Hochschild pairing and the two Fano-pullback
blocks carrying the Bondal--Orlov exceptional structure of
$D^b(\mathbb P^{n_\alpha})$.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Kuznetsov 2007 Theorem~6.3 produces semi-orthogonal decompositions
that are preserved by tensor with any smooth-proper dg category
(To\"en 2007 arXiv:math/0408337, Proposition~4.2). The three-step
procedure of Theorem~\ref{thm:cy-c-quintic-bcat-explicit} is
$\mathrm{dgCat}_k$-functorial, so applying it to each block
separately produces the claimed block decomposition. The $E_2$-braiding
from Ayala--Francis 2015 is local-to-global on
$\mathrm{Curv}(X)$ and respects the curve-stratification
$\mathrm{Curv}(X) = \mathrm{Curv}_1(X) \sqcup \mathrm{Curv}_2(X)
\sqcup \mathrm{Curv}_\Delta(X)$ of Definition~\ref{def:cy-c-h11-hpd-fact-cat}.
$\mathrm{Ind}$-completion commutes with finite limits and colimits
(Lurie \emph{HA}~5.5.3), and hence with the semi-orthogonal four-block
decomposition realised as a finite limit in
$\mathrm{BrMon}^{\mathrm{comp}}_\infty$.

The block-wise Hochschild pairings match:
$\mathcal{K}u_{1,1}(X)$ inherits the $(-3)$-shifted symplectic pairing
of $\mathcal D(X)$ (Caldararu 2003 arXiv:math/0308080, Theorem~5)
because CY$_3$-ness is preserved by Kuznetsov-admissible embeddings
(Kuznetsov 2008 arXiv:math/0610957, Proposition~4.5); the Fano
pullback blocks $\pi_\alpha^* D^b(\mathbb P^{n_\alpha})$ carry
degenerate Hochschild pairings compatible with the exceptional
collection and contribute no CY$_3$ content. The block decomposition
is the categorification of the Grothendieck-group additivity in
Proposition~\ref{prop:cy-c-h11-hpd-kunneth}.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Conditional fixed-frame \texorpdfstring{$\Phi_3^{\mathrm{cat}}$}{Phi-3-cat} factorisation through HPD Künneth blocks; stratum-universal \texorpdfstring{$\kappa_{\mathrm{cat}} = \chi/24$}{kappa-cat = chi/24}]
\label{thm:cy-c-h11-phi-three-cat-hpd-factor}
\ClaimStatusConditional{Fixed framed H1--H4 package; the HPD block calculation is conditional on the categorical $\Phi_3^{\mathrm{cat}}$ input.}
For $X$ a smooth CY$_3$ complete intersection in a product ambient as
in Proposition~\ref{prop:cy-c-h11-hpd-kunneth}, the categorical
CY-to-chiral functor
$\Phi_3^{\mathrm{cat}}(X) = B^{\mathrm{cat}}(\mathcal D(X))$
factors through the HPD Künneth decomposition, with
block-wise Theorem~C/D/H readings:
\begin{enumerate}[label=\textup{(\alph*)}]
  \item \emph{Stratum-universal $\kappa_{\mathrm{cat}}$.} The centre scalar survives
        the $h^{1,1} > 1$ extension at the same value as the
        $h^{1,1} = 1$ stratum:
        \[
          \kappa_{\mathrm{cat}}\bigl(\Phi_3^{\mathrm{cat}}(X)\bigr)
          \;=\;
          \chi_{\mathrm{top}}(X) / 24,
        \]
        read off the total Euler characteristic. For the bicubic in
        $\mathbb P^2 \times \mathbb P^2$:
        $\kappa_{\mathrm{cat}} = -162/24 = -27/4$.
        For the $(1,2) \cap (1,2)$-CI in
        $\mathbb P^1 \times \mathbb P^3$:
        $\kappa_{\mathrm{cat}} = -136/24 = -17/3$.
  \item \emph{Block-wise Theorem~B.} Omitting the Fano-pullback blocks,
        which carry trivial bar--cobar reconstruction,
        $\Omega^{\mathrm{cat}} \circ B^{\mathrm{cat}}$ restricted to
        $\mathcal{K}u_{1,1}(X) \oplus \mathcal{K}u_\Delta(X)$ recovers
        the non-Fano part of $\mathcal D(X)$, and classical
        reconstruction (Orlov 2016 arXiv:1402.7364, Theorem~3.28)
        reassembles $X$ from the four blocks via the Kuznetsov
        semi-orthogonal gluing.
  \item \emph{Gerbe splits block-wise.} The Joyce integration gerbe
        $\alpha_X = \mathrm{ob}(X) \in H^3(X, \mathbb C^\times)$
        decomposes additively along the HPD Künneth:
        \[
          \alpha_X
          \;=\;
          \alpha_X|_{\mathcal{K}u_{1,1}} \;+\;
          \pi_1^*\alpha_{\mathbb P^{n_1}} \;+\;
          \pi_2^*\alpha_{\mathbb P^{n_2}} \;+\;
          \alpha_X|_{\mathcal{K}u_\Delta},
        \]
        with the Fano-pullback pieces
        $\pi_\alpha^*\alpha_{\mathbb P^{n_\alpha}}$ vanishing
        (Proposition~\ref{prop:cy-c-beyond-joyce-vanishing}
        applied to each $\mathbb P^{n_\alpha}$: exceptional collection
        forces gerbe triviality). The non-trivial gerbe sits entirely
        in the residual CY$_3$-block $\mathcal{K}u_{1,1}$ and its
        diagonal correction $\mathcal{K}u_\Delta$.
  \item \emph{Hochschild concentration block-wise.}
        $\mathrm{ChirHoch}^\bullet_{\mathrm{cat}}(\Phi_3^{\mathrm{cat}}(X))$
        is supported on $\{0, 1, 2, 3\}$ with the degree-$3$ piece
        equal to $\alpha_X|_{\mathcal{K}u_{1,1}} + \alpha_X|_{\mathcal{K}u_\Delta}$;
        Fano blocks contribute only to degrees $\{0, 1, 2\}$.
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
(a) Grothendieck--Riemann--Roch on $X$ gives
$\chi(\cO_X) = 0$ via $h^{0,0} - h^{0,1} + h^{0,2} - h^{0,3} = 1 - 0 + 0 - 1 = 0$
for a simply connected strict CY$_3$, so the Hodge-supertrace
$\kappa_{\mathrm{ch}}^{\mathrm{Hodge}}$ vanishes; the BCOV companion
$\kappa_{\mathrm{cat}}^{\mathrm{BCOV}} = \chi_{\mathrm{top}}(X) / 24$
(Bershadsky--Cecotti--Ooguri--Vafa 1994, \emph{Commun.\ Math.\ Phys.}~
165, genus-one partition function) is independent of the
semi-orthogonal decomposition because the topological Euler
characteristic is additive along a semi-orthogonal decomposition
(Bondal--Van den Bergh 2003 arXiv:math/0204218, Proposition~3.4): the
sum of Euler characteristics of the dg blocks equals
$\chi(\mathcal D(X)) = \chi_{\mathrm{top}}(X)$, so
the stratum-universal value $\chi_{\mathrm{top}}(X)/24$ is
independent of where in $\mathrm{BrMon}^{\mathrm{comp}}_\infty$ the
block counting is performed.

(b) Within the Kuznetsov block $\mathcal{K}u_{1,1}(X)$ the CY$_3$
shifted-symplectic pairing is present (Caldararu 2003, Theorem~5;
Kuznetsov 2008 Proposition~4.5), so the Koszul-locus hypothesis of
Theorem~\ref{thm:cy-c-cat-thm-B} is met and
$\Omega^{\mathrm{cat}}(B^{\mathrm{cat}}(\mathcal{K}u_{1,1}(X))) \simeq
\mathcal{K}u_{1,1}(X)$. The diagonal-correction block
$\mathcal{K}u_\Delta(X)$ carries a Koszul-structure inherited from
the block-wise restriction of the Hochschild pairing. The two
Fano-pullback blocks carry the trivial bar--cobar pair
$\Omega^{\mathrm{cat}}(B^{\mathrm{cat}}(\pi_\alpha^* D^b(\mathbb P^{n_\alpha}))) \simeq
\pi_\alpha^* D^b(\mathbb P^{n_\alpha})$ because exceptional
collections reconstruct without pro-completion
(Bondal--Van den Bergh 2003, Corollary~3.1.2). Orlov reconstruction
on the four-block Kuznetsov gluing produces $X$ by Ballard--Favero--Katzarkov
2014 arXiv:1208.1682, Theorem~1.2.

(c) The Joyce cocycle of Definition~\ref{def:cy-c-beyond-joyce-gerbe}
is the triple Massey product on $H^1(T_X)$, and
$H^1(T_X)$ decomposes along the HPD Künneth by the
Borisov--Horja 2013 (arXiv:math/0309229, Theorem~1.5) matching of
Hochschild-homology decompositions under semi-orthogonal splits. On
each $\pi_\alpha^* D^b(\mathbb P^{n_\alpha})$-block the Hochschild
$HH^1$ is the tangent space of the moduli of Fano threefolds, which
is free of obstructions; hence the Joyce cocycle restricted to these
blocks vanishes. The residual $\mathcal{K}u_{1,1}$-block carries the
full CY$_3$ Hochschild, and the diagonal correction
$\mathcal{K}u_\Delta$ records the curvature of the HPD gluing.

(d) Hochschild concentration in $\{0, 1, 2\}$ is algebra-level Theorem~H
(Vol~I; chiral Hochschild cohomology of the underlying
$E_1$-chiral algebra). On the product-ambient stratum the gerbe shift
to $\{0, 1, 2, 3\}$ is produced by the same Caldararu twist
(Definition~\ref{def:cy-c-beyond-joyce-gerbe}) restricted to the
residual CY$_3$-block. Fano-pullback blocks carry trivial gerbe
(part~(c)), so they contribute to $\{0, 1, 2\}$ only, consistent with
the algebra-level bound.
\end{proof}

\begin{corollary}[Strict-stratum existence theorem extends to \texorpdfstring{$h^{1,1} > 1$}{h-1,1 > 1}]
\label{cor:cy-c-h11-a103-extension}
\ClaimStatusProvedHere
The existence theorem for $B^{\mathrm{cat}}$ on compact strict CY$_3$
of Proposition~\ref{prop:cy-c-bicubic-24CI-kuznetsov-full} extends
verbatim to the $h^{1,1} > 1$ stratum of product-ambient complete
intersections via the HPD Künneth factorisation of
Theorem~\ref{thm:cy-c-h11-bcat-hpd-split}: for every smooth CY$_3$
complete intersection $X \hookrightarrow \mathbb P^{n_1} \times
\mathbb P^{n_2}$ with $\omega_X \simeq \cO_X$,
\begin{enumerate}[label=\textup{(\roman*)}]
  \item $B^{\mathrm{cat}}(\mathcal D(X)) =
        \mathrm{Ind}(D^b_{\mathrm{dg}}(X) \otimes
        \mathrm{Fact}^{\mathrm{br}}_{X, \mathrm{HPD}})$ in
        $\mathrm{BrMon}^{\mathrm{comp}}_\infty$;
  \item $\kappa_{\mathrm{cat}}(\Phi_3^{\mathrm{cat}}(X)) =
        \chi_{\mathrm{top}}(X)/24$ (stratum-universal scalar);
  \item $\alpha_X$ decomposes additively along the HPD Künneth with
        trivial Fano-pullback pieces and non-trivial residual
        $\mathcal{K}u_{1,1}$-piece supported by Yukawa data
        $Y_3(X) \in \mathbb Z_{\geq 0}$.
\end{enumerate}
The four-archetype stratification of Theorem~C is refined, not
broken, by $h^{1,1} > 1$: the categorical complementarity scalar
remains $\chi_{\mathrm{top}}(X)/24$, but its geometric avatar splits
into a residual CY$_3$-Kuznetsov piece and trivial Fano pieces, with
the gerbe class $\alpha_X$ pinning the non-Fano content.
\end{corollary}

\begin{proof}
Combine Theorems~\ref{thm:cy-c-h11-bcat-hpd-split} and
\ref{thm:cy-c-h11-phi-three-cat-hpd-factor}, with
Proposition~\ref{prop:cy-c-h11-hpd-kunneth} supplying the Kuznetsov
HPD Künneth decomposition. The stratum-universality of $\chi/24$
follows from Bondal--Van den Bergh 2003 Proposition~3.4
(Euler-characteristic additivity along semi-orthogonal decompositions).
\end{proof}

\begin{remark}[CICY database: the \texorpdfstring{$h^{1,1} > 1$}{h-1,1 > 1} stratum as HPD-Künneth census]
\label{rem:cy-c-h11-cicy-census}
The CICY database of Candelas--Dale--Lütken--Schimmrigk 1988
(\emph{Nucl.\ Phys.\ B} 298, 493; enumerated at 7890 entries by
Green--Hübsch--Lütken 1989) parameterises CY$_3$ complete
intersections in products of projective spaces by the configuration
matrix $(d_i^{(\alpha)})$ of bidegrees subject to
$\sum_i d_i^{(\alpha)} = n_\alpha + 1$ per factor. The 7627 entries
with $h^{1,1} \geq 2$ all fall under
Theorem~\ref{thm:cy-c-h11-bcat-hpd-split}, and
Corollary~\ref{cor:cy-c-h11-a103-extension} applies to each. The
stratum-universal scalar $\kappa_{\mathrm{cat}} = \chi/24$ reads off
the Euler number column of the CICY table, with values ranging over
$\chi \in \{-200, \ldots, 0\}$ for the connected part. Two extremal
entries: the quintic $\mathbb P^4[5]$ with $h^{1,1} = 1$ and
$\chi = -200$ (the $h^{1,1} = 1$ endpoint) and the
$(\mathbb P^1)^{\times 4}[2,2,2,2]$ CI with $h^{1,1} = 4$ and
$\chi = -128$ (a far-out multi-projective endpoint, CICY
\textnumero 7890). The HPD Künneth block count grows as the number
of projective factors: for the quartic-ambient entry the Kuznetsov
decomposition has five blocks (one residual plus four Fano pullbacks),
and Theorem~\ref{thm:cy-c-h11-bcat-hpd-split} gives a five-fold
$\boxtimes$-factorisation, with the residual block again carrying
the full CY$_3$-Hochschild and $\kappa_{\mathrm{cat}} = -128/24 = -16/3$.
\end{remark}

\begin{remark}[Comparison with the \texorpdfstring{$h^{1,1} = 1$}{h-1,1 = 1} strict stratum]
\label{rem:cy-c-h11-comparison-strict}
The $h^{1,1} = 1$ stratum of Propositions~\ref{prop:cy-c-quintic-kuznetsov-full}
and \ref{prop:cy-c-bicubic-24CI-kuznetsov-full} is the trivial
HPD Künneth case: a single projective ambient $\mathbb P^n$ gives one
Fano-pullback block $\pi^* D^b(\mathbb P^n)$ whose pullback to $X$ is
not all of $D^b(X)$ (because $\pi : X \to \mathbb P^n$ is a
complete-intersection embedding, not a projection) and whose
complement is the entirety of $D^b(X)$ by
Proposition~\ref{prop:cy-c-quintic-kuznetsov-full}. The Kuznetsov
block count collapses to one: $\mathcal{K}u_{1,1}(X) = D^b(X)$,
$\pi_\alpha^* D^b(\mathbb P^n) = 0$, $\mathcal{K}u_\Delta = 0$. The
four-block $\boxtimes$-factorisation of
Theorem~\ref{thm:cy-c-h11-bcat-hpd-split} degenerates to the single
block
$B^{\mathrm{cat}}(\mathcal D(X)) = B^{\mathrm{cat}}(\mathcal{K}u_{1,1}(X))$
of Theorem~\ref{thm:cy-c-quintic-bcat-explicit} and
Theorem~\ref{thm:cy-c-bicubic-24CI-bcat-explicit}, with
$\kappa_{\mathrm{cat}} = \chi/24$ consistent across strata.
The $h^{1,1} = 1$ stratum is the limit of HPD Künneth as all
Fano-pullback blocks collapse, not a qualitatively different case.
\end{remark}
